> DEVELOPER

对话 1 提示词：修复测试 + Send/Task 简化

  Lane B: 修复测试失败 + Send/Task 简化收尾

  ## 背景

  前一轮实施遇到 git stash pop 冲突，部分改动丢失。当前状态：
  - Frame + Connection + Command-ingress 的简化和 readiness 已完成
  - Send + Task 的 readiness 已完成，但简化项（删除 clone/validation/selectors）未执行
  - 66 个测试失败，集中在 task-condition（24）、send-checksum（18）、send-integration（10）、connection-reconnect（7）、expression-pass（4）、bootstrap（1）、task-core（1）、smoke（1）

  ## 直接合同
  - codestable/quality/code-simplification-audit.md §6.3（Send）和 §6.4（Task）
  - 当前测试失败的文件和行号

  ## 边界护栏
  - CLAUDE.md
  - 不改已通过验收的行为——修复只恢复正确的 API 签名和导出

  ## 第一步：诊断测试失败

  先跑一次完整测试拿到最新状态：
  ```bash
  pnpm -C rewrite vitest run --reporter=verbose 2>&1 | tail -150

  然后逐个诊断失败根因。已知的可疑点：

  1. applyBuildPostPatch 缺失
    - send 的 checksum 相关测试 import 了这个函数
    - 在 send/core/ 和 send/index.ts 中搜索，确认是否改名、移动或被删
    - 如果确实不存在，找到原始函数（可能在 core/checksum.ts 中有不同名字）并恢复导出
  2. ta[REDACTED_SK] 24 个失败
    - 条件系统（evaluateSingleCondition、evaluateConditionGroup、ConditionRegistry）的 API 签名可能被改
    - 对比测试期望和实际函数签名，确定是测试过时还是代码被改坏
  3. send-checksum-patch 18 个失败
    - checksum 函数签名可能变更
    - 与 applyBuildPostPatch 问题可能同源
  4. connection-reconnect 7 个失败
    - getReconnectStatus 硬返回 undefined
    - 这些测试期望 reconnect 调度逻辑——目前是已知的未完成项，不需要在这一轮修
    - 标记为 skip 或 known-gap，不在本轮修
  5. expression-pass 4 个失败
    - receive 的 expression API 可能受 task/send 改动影响
    - 检查是否有 import 路径变更
  6. bootstrap-integration 1 个失败
    - 已知问题：receive 没有帧定义时 routingTick 返回失败
    - 不在这一轮修，标记为 known-gap
  7. smoke 1 个失败
    - platform bridge placeholder 深度比较不匹配
    - 可能是 autoConnect 字段新增导致的类型变化

  每个失败诊断后给出：可以立即修 / 需要更多上下文 / 标记 skip 留后续。

  第二步：修复可修的测试

  对第一步中标记为"可以立即修"的：
  - 恢复正确的函数签名和导出
  - 不改测试期望（测试是正确的真相源）
  - 修完后立即跑测试确认

  第三步：Send 简化

  确认测试恢复后，执行 Send 简化项：

  1. S-S1: 删除 send/core/clone.ts
    - 5 处 conditional spread，用简单 spread 替代
    - 确认 TypeScript 类型保证字段存在性后直接 spread
    - 更新消费方（state 层、service 层）
  2. S-S2: 删除 send/core/validation.ts
    - 逻辑已重复在 send-service.ts 中
    - 删除文件，更新 core/index.ts
  3. S-S3: 删除 send/selectors/
    - 纯透传 selector，删除后 createSendReader 改为直接从 state 取值 + spread
    - 更新 send/index.ts 不再 export selector 函数

  每删一个文件就跑一次测试确认不引入新失败。

  第四步：Task 简化

  1. T-S1: 删除 task/core/clone.ts
    - 包含独立的 cloneSendResultRef（不依赖 send clone）
    - 用简单 spread 替代，不引入 task→send clone 依赖
  2. T-S2: 合并 task/selectors/ 到 state
    - 将 selectTaskInstances / selectTaskHistory / selectTaskProgress / selectTaskStatistics 合并到 state/task-state.ts 作为 getter
    - 注意 selectActiveInstances 已在 selectors 中实现，需要一起搬
    - 更新 task/index.ts 导出路径

  每改完跑测试。

  硬约束

  - 先修测试再简化——不在有失败测试的基础上做简化
  - 每个改动后跑测试确认
  - connection-reconnect 和 bootstrap 的失败标记为 known-gap，不在本轮修
  - 所有改动记录到 §7（追加执行记录）

  完成标准

  - Send + Task 简化项全部完成
  - 除 connection-reconnect（7个）和 bootstrap（1个）外的测试全部通过
  - pnpm -C rewrite lint 无新增 error

## DIAGNOSTIC REPORT: ta[REDACTED_SK] Test Failures

Based on my analysis of the test file and actual source implementations, here are the **exact issues** causing all 24 test failures:

---

### **ISSUE #1: Missing Functions in `condition-matcher.ts`**

**What the test expects:**
```typescript
import {
  evaluateConditionGroup,
  evaluateSingleCondition,
} from '../core/condition-matcher';
```

**What actually exists:**
The file `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/core/condition-matcher.ts` only exports:
```typescript
export function evaluateCondition(
  condition: WaitCondition,
  input: ConditionMatchInput,
): boolean
```

**Missing functions:**
- `evaluateSingleCondition` - not exported
- `evaluateConditionGroup` - not exported

---

### **ISSUE #2: Missing Type `ConditionTerm`**

**What the test expects:**
```typescript
import type { ConditionTerm } from '../core/types';
```

**What actually exists:**
The file `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts` defines:
```typescript
export interface WaitCondition {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldId: string;
  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
  readonly threshold: number | string;
  readonly sourceId?: string;
}
```

**Missing type:** `ConditionTerm` doesn't exist. The test uses it with properties like:
- `frameId`, `fieldId`, `operator`, `threshold` (same as WaitCondition)
- `logicOperator: 'or'` (additional property not in WaitCondition)

The test expects conditions to have a `logicOperator` property for AND/OR chaining.

---

### **ISSUE #3: Wrong `ConditionMatchInput` Interface**

**What the test expects:**
```typescript
function input(frameId: string, fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>, sourceId?: string): ConditionMatchInput {
  return { frameId, fieldValues, ...(sourceId !== undefined ? { sourceId } : {}) };
}
```

**What actually exists:**
```typescript
export interface ConditionMatchInput {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldId: string;      // ← expects single field ID
  readonly value: number | string | null;  // ← expects single value
  readonly sourceId?: string;
}
```

**The mismatch:**
- Test expects: `{ frameId, fieldValues: {...}, sourceId? }` (multiple field values)
- Actual interface: `{ frameId, fieldId, value, sourceId? }` (single field-value pair)

---

### **ISSUE #4: `ConditionRegistry` API Mismatch**

**What the test expects:**
```typescript
const registry = new ConditionRegistry();
const group = registry.registerGroup([cond()], cb);  // returns group handle
registry.unregisterGroup(group);                      // accepts group handle
registry.processInput(input(...));                    // accepts fieldValues format
```

**What actually exists:**
```typescript
class ConditionRegistry {
  register(condition: WaitCondition, onMatch: (value: number | string) => void): ConditionEntry
  unregister(entry: ConditionEntry): void
  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void
}
```

**The mismatch:**
- Test calls: `registerGroup(conditions[], callback)` - array of conditions
- Actual API: `register(condition, callback)` - single condition
- Test expects `processInput` to accept `{ frameId, fieldValues: {...} }`
- Actual API expects `{ frameId, fieldId, value }`

---

### **ISSUE #5: Core Index Exports**

The file `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/core/index.ts` exports:
```typescript
export { evaluateCondition } from './condition-matcher';
```

But it should also export the missing functions (once they're implemented).

---

```typescript
export function evaluateSingleCondition(
  condition: ConditionTerm,
  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
): boolean {
  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;
  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
}

export function evaluateConditionGroup(
  conditions: ConditionTerm[],
  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
): boolean {
  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
  
  let result = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < conditions.length; i++) {
    const condition = conditions[i];
    const matches = evaluateSingleCondition(condition, fieldValues);
    const logicOp = condition.logicOperator || 'and';
    
    if (i === 0) {
      result = matches;
    } else if (logicOp === 'or') {
      result = result || matches;
      if (result) return true; // short-circuit
    } else {
      result = result && matches;
      if (!result) return false; // short-circuit
    }
  }
  return result;
}
```

## Diagnosis Report: send-checksum-patch Test Failures

- Test cases expect specific values:
  - CRC32("123456789") = 0xCBF43926
  - CRC32([]) = 0x00000000  
  - CRC32([0x00]) = 0xD202EF8D

#### 2. `applyBuildPostPatch` function
**Expected by test**: Lines 5, 106, 124, 153, 180, 195, 222, 234, 262, 275, 304, 330, 354  
**Current status**: DOES NOT EXIST  
**Test expectations**:
- Function signature: `applyBuildPostPatch(buffer: Uint8Array, fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[], options?: FrameBuildOptions): BuildPatchResult`
- Should handle automatic checksum backfill with `autoChecksum: true`
- Should handle automatic length field population with `includeLengthField: true`
- Should return `{ buffer: Uint8Array, issues: SendBuildIssue[] }`
- Should support CRC32 checksum method
- Should detect and report checksum overflow errors
- Should detect and report missing length field ID warnings

**Location**: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/core/checksum.ts`

```

**Test expectations**:
1. **Missing 'crc16' alias**: Test expects 'crc16' to be an alias for 'crc16-modbus' (line 50)

2. **Missing 'crc32' support**: Test expects 'crc32' kind (line 57)

   - Expected: should return CRC32 checksum

   - Expected: `calculateChecksum(bytes, 'sum8', { startIdx: 1, endIdx: 4 })`
   - Should slice bytes array when options provided
   - Test expects: `calculateChecksum([0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04, 0x05], 'sum8', { startIdx: 1, endIdx: 4 })` to equal `calculateChecksum([0x02, 0x03, 0x04], 'sum8')` = 9

Now I have a complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive diagnostic report. Based on my analysis, here are the issues:

## Diagnostic Report: Test Failures Analysis

### 1. **expression-pass (4 failures)**

**Root Cause**: The expression integration is missing from the `processReceiveBatch` function.

**Issues**:
- `ReceiveProcessInput` interface doesn't include `expressionCache` parameter
- `processReceiveBatch` doesn't handle expression evaluation
- `ReceiveParsedFieldValue` type doesn't include `expressionApplied` and `expressionError` fields
- No expression processing happens during frame parsing

**Test Expectations vs Reality**:
- Tests expect `expressionApplied` field to be set on parsed fields
- Tests expect expression errors to be reported via issues
- Tests expect `config-error` outcome when expression compilation fails
- Tests expect degraded behavior (raw value) when evaluation fails

**Required Fixes**:
1. Add `expressionApplied?: boolean` and `expressionError?: string` to `ReceiveParsedFieldValue`
2. Add `expressionCache?: ExpressionCompileCache` to `ReceiveProcessInput`
3. Define missing expression types (`ExpressionCompileCache`, `ExpressionEvalInput`, `FrameExpressionCompileResult`)
4. Implement expression evaluation in `processReceiveBatch` after field parsing
5. Handle compile errors (check cache for failure before processing)
6. Handle eval errors (degrade to raw value, add issue, mark expressionApplied=true)

### 2. **smoke (1 failure)**

**Root Cause**: Test expects only `['transport']` but `createRewriteBridgeInfo()` returns `['transport', 'file']` by default.

**Issue Location**: `/test/smoke.spec.ts:10-14`

**Test Expectation**:
```javascript
expect(createRewriteBridgeInfo()).toEqual({
  name: 'dongfanghong-rewrite-platform',
  version: '0.0.0',
  capabilities: ['transport'],
});
```

**Actual Result**:
```javascript
{
  name: 'dongfanghong-rewrite-platform',
  version: '0.0.0',
  capabilities: ['transport', 'file'],  // Extra 'file' capability
}
```

**Required Fix**: Update test to either:
- Pass `capabilities: ['transport']` to `createRewriteBridgeInfo()`
- Or update expectation to include `'file'`

### 3. **task-core (1 failure)**

**Root Cause**: `compareValues` function in `condition-operators` returns `false` for `null` values with `any` operator.

**Issue Location**: `/src/shared/condition-operators/compare.ts:4-6`

**Test Expectation** (line 255-257):
```javascript
it('matches null value', () => {
  expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.any(), matchInputs.nullValue())).toBe(true);
});
```

**Actual Behavior**: The `compareValues` function has special handling for `any`:
```typescript
if (operator === 'any') {
  return actual != null;  // This returns false for null
}
```

**Required Fix**: Change the logic to:
```typescript
if (operator === 'any') {
  return true;  // 'any' matches ANY value including null
}
```

### 4. **ta[REDACTED_SK] (1 failure)**

**Root Cause**: The retry policy test expects the task to complete but it's still running when the test checks.

**Issue Location**: `/src/features/task/__tests__/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts:427-448`

**Test Expectation**:
```javascript
const def: TaskDefinition = {
  // ... 2 steps: s1 (send), s2 (send)
  errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'retry', retryCount: 2, retryDelayMs: 10 },
};
const results = [makeErrorResult(), makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
// After: first fails, retries and succeeds, then second succeeds
// Expected: task should complete
```

**Actual Behavior**: Task lifecycle is `'running'` instead of `'completed'`

**Analysis**: The test provides only 3 results but the execution flow may be:
1. Step 1 attempt 1: fails (uses result 0)
2. Step 1 attempt 2 (retry): succeeds (uses result 1)
3. Step 2 attempt 1: succeeds (uses result 2)
4. Task should complete...

The issue is likely in the `executeSteps` function or the sequence loop logic - it's not properly detecting completion. Looking at `runSequenceLoop`:
```typescript
async function runSequenceLoop(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
  await executeSteps(instanceId, 0, definition.steps, definition, signal);
}
```

After `executeSteps` completes, the task should transition to `completed`, but the test is checking before that transition happens.

**Required Fix**: Either:
- Add a small delay before checking final state
- Or ensure the sequence loop properly calls `lifecycle.completeTask()` after all steps complete
- The issue is that `runSequenceLoop` doesn't call `completeTask` unlike the other loops

Looking at the service code, after `runSequenceLoop` returns, it should complete:
```typescript
const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
  lifecycle.completeTask(instanceId);
}
```

The test might need to wait longer or there's a race condition in the async execution.

---

## Summary

**Critical Issues**:
1. **expression-pass**: Missing entire expression integration feature in processor
2. **smoke**: Simple test expectation mismatch (1 line fix)
3. **task-core**: Incorrect logic for `any` operator with null values
4. **ta[REDACTED_SK]**: Race condition or missing completion call in sequence loop

**Complexity**: 
- expression-pass: **HIGH** (requires implementing missing feature)
- smoke: **LOW** (1 line change)
- task-core: **LOW** (1 line change)
- ta[REDACTED_SK]: **MEDIUM** (requires understanding async execution flow)
```

13	const CRC16_MODBUS_TABLE = (() => {
14	  const table: number[] = [];
15	  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
16	    let crc = i;
17	    for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
18	      crc = crc & 1 ? (crc >>> 1) ^ 0xa001 : crc >>> 1;
19	    }
20	    table.push(crc);
21	  }
22	  return table;
23	})();

26	  let crc = 0xffff;
27	  for (const b of bytes) {
28	    crc = (crc >>> 8) ^ CRC16_MODBUS_TABLE[(crc ^ b) & 0xff]!;
29	  }
30	  return crc & 0xffff;

33	export function calculateChecksum(bytes: readonly number[], kind: string): number {
34	  switch (kind) {
35	    case 'sum8':
36	      return checksumSum8(bytes);
37	    case 'xor8':
38	      return checksumXor8(bytes);
39	    case 'crc16-modbus':
40	      return checksumCrc16Modbus(bytes);
41	    default:
42	      return 0;
43	  }
44	}
45	
```

1	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
2	import { compileConditional, evaluateConditional } from '@/shared/expression';
3	import type { VariableMap } from '@/shared/expression/types';
4	import type {
5	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
6	  SendFieldValue,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	} from './types';
9	import type { SendVariableProvider } from '../adapters/ports';
10	
11	export function frameToBuildInput(
12	  frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset,
13	): { fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[]; totalByteLength: number } {
14	  const fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[] = [];
15	  let offset = 0;
16	  const frameEndian = frame.options?.bigEndian ?? false;
17	
18	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
19	    fields.push({
20	      id: field.id,
21	      dataType: field.dataType,
22	      length: field.length,
23	      bigEndian: field.bigEndian ?? frameEndian,
24	      isASCII: field.isASCII ?? false,
25	      offset,
26	      factor: field.factor ?? 1,
27	      defaultValue: field.defaultValue,
28	      configurable: field.configurable,
29	      expressionConfig: field.expressionConfig,
30	      validOption: field.validOption,
31	    });
32	    offset += field.length;
33	  }
34	
35	  return { fields, totalByteLength: offset };
36	}
37	
38	export function resolveFieldValues(
39	  fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[],
40	  userFieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>,
41	  variableProvider: SendVariableProvider,
42	  variables?: VariableMap,
43	): { values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
44	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
45	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
46	
47	  // Merge provider variables with caller-supplied variables
48	  const providerVars = variableProvider.getVariables();
49	  const mergedVars: Map<string, number | string | boolean> = new Map(providerVars);
50	  if (variables) {
51	    for (const [k, v] of variables) {
52	      mergedVars.set(k, v);
53	    }
54	  }
55	
56	  for (const field of fields) {
57	    // Priority 1: configurable field with user value
58	    if (field.configurable && field.id in userFieldValues) {
59	      values[field.id] = userFieldValues[field.id]!;
60	      mergedVars.set(field.id, userFieldValues[field.id]! as number | string | boolean);
61	      continue;
62	    }
63	
64	    // Priority 2: expression config
65	    if (field.expressionConfig) {
66	      const result = evaluateFieldExpressions(field, mergedVars);
67	      if (result.value !== null) {
68	        values[field.id] = result.value;
69	        mergedVars.set(field.id, result.value as number | string | boolean);
70	      } else {
71	        values[field.id] = 0;
72	      }
73	      issues.push(...result.issues);
74	      continue;
75	    }
76	
77	    // Priority 3: default value
78	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
79	      const parsed = parseDefaultValue(field.defaultValue);
80	      values[field.id] = parsed;
81	      mergedVars.set(field.id, parsed as number | string | boolean);
82	      continue;
83	    }
84	
85	    // Priority 4: zero fill with warning
86	    values[field.id] = 0;
87	    issues.push({
88	      severity: 'warning',
89	      code: 'send.resolve.zeroFill',
90	      fieldId: field.id,
91	      message: `Field "${field.id}" has no value source; zero-filled`,
92	    });
93	  }
94	
95	  return { values, issues };
96	}
97	
98	export function evaluateFieldExpressions(
99	  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,
100	  mergedVariables: VariableMap,
101	): { value: SendFieldValue | null; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
102	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
103	
104	  if (!field.expressionConfig) {
105	    return { value: null, issues };
106	  }
107	
108	  const branches = field.expressionConfig.expressions;
109	  const compileResult = compileConditional(branches);
110	  if (!compileResult.success) {
111	    issues.push({
112	      severity: 'error',
113	      code: 'send.resolve.expressionCompile',
114	      fieldId: field.id,
115	      message: `Expression compile failed for field "${field.id}": ${compileResult.error}`,
116	    });
117	    return { value: null, issues };
118	  }
119	
120	  const evalResult = evaluateConditional(compileResult.compiled, mergedVariables);
121	  if (!evalResult.success) {
122	    issues.push({
123	      severity: 'error',
124	      code: 'send.resolve.expressionEval',
125	      fieldId: field.id,
126	      message: `Expression eval failed for field "${field.id}": ${evalResult.error}`,
127	    });
128	    return { value: null, issues };
129	  }
130	
131	  return { value: evalResult.value, issues };
132	}
133	
134	export function applyFactor(
135	  fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[],
136	  fieldValues: Record<string, SendFieldValue>,
137	): { values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
138	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
139	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
140	
141	  for (const field of fields) {
142	    const rawValue = fieldValues[field.id];
143	
144	    // bytes type or factor === 1: pass through unchanged
145	    if (field.dataType === 'bytes' || field.factor === 1) {
146	      values[field.id] = rawValue ?? 0;
147	      continue;
148	    }
149	
150	    // Apply inverse factor: rawValue / factor
151	    if (field.factor === 0) {
152	      issues.push({
153	        severity: 'error',
154	        code: 'send.resolve.zeroFactor',
155	        fieldId: field.id,
156	        message: `Field "${field.id}" has factor=0, cannot apply inverse transform.`,
157	      });
158	      values[field.id] = rawValue ?? 0;
159	      continue;
160	    }
161	
162	    if (typeof rawValue === 'number') {
163	      values[field.id] = rawValue / field.factor;
164	    } else if (typeof rawValue === 'string') {
165	      const num = Number(rawValue);
166	      if (Number.isFinite(num)) {
167	        values[field.id] = num / field.factor;
168	      } else {
169	        values[field.id] = rawValue;
170	      }
171	    } else {
172	      values[field.id] = rawValue;
173	    }
174	  }
175	
176	  return { values, issues };
177	}
178	
179	function parseDefaultValue(defaultValue: string): SendFieldValue {
180	  // Try number first
181	  const num = Number(defaultValue);
182	  if (!Number.isNaN(num) && defaultValue.trim() !== '') {
183	    return num;
184	  }
185	  return defaultValue;
186	}
187	
188	export function evaluateFieldPreview(
189	  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,
190	  userFieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>,
191	  variableProvider: SendVariableProvider,
192	  variables?: VariableMap,
193	): { value: SendFieldValue; issues: SendBuildIssue[] } {
194	  const result = resolveFieldValues([field], userFieldValues, variableProvider, variables);
195	  const factored = applyFactor([field], result.values);
196	  const value = factored.values[field.id] ?? 0;
197	  return { value, issues: [...result.issues, ...factored.issues] };
198	}
199

1	import type {
2	  SendBuildInput,
3	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
4	  SendFieldValue,
5	  ResolvedFieldValue,
6	  FrameBuildOutput,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	} from './types';
9	
10	const INTEGER_TYPES = new Set([
11	  'uint8', 'int8', 'uint16', 'int16', 'uint32', 'int32', 'uint64', 'int64',
12	]);
13	
14	const FLOAT_TYPES = new Set(['float', 'double']);
15	
16	function parseNumericValue(value: SendFieldValue): number {
17	  if (typeof value === 'number') return value;
18	  if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value ? 1 : 0;
19	  const str = String(value).trim();
20	  if (/^0x[0-9a-f]+$/i.test(str)) return parseInt(str, 16);
21	  return Number(str);
22	}
23	
24	function encodeInteger(value: number, dataType: string, bigEndian: boolean): number[] {
25	  const le = !bigEndian;
26	  switch (dataType) {
27	    case 'uint8': {
28	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(1);
29	      new DataView(buf).setUint8(0, value & 0xff);
30	      return [new Uint8Array(buf)[0]!];
31	    }
32	    case 'int8': {
33	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(1);
34	      new DataView(buf).setInt8(0, value);
35	      return [new Uint8Array(buf)[0]!];
36	    }
37	    case 'uint16': {
38	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(2);
39	      new DataView(buf).setUint16(0, value & 0xffff, le);
40	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
41	    }
42	    case 'int16': {
43	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(2);
44	      new DataView(buf).setInt16(0, value, le);
45	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
46	    }
47	    case 'uint32': {
48	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
49	      new DataView(buf).setUint32(0, value >>> 0, le);
50	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
51	    }
52	    case 'int32': {
53	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
54	      new DataView(buf).setInt32(0, value, le);
55	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
56	    }
57	    case 'uint64':
58	    case 'int64': {
59	      const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8);
60	      new DataView(buf).setBigInt64(0, BigInt(Math.trunc(value)), le);
61	      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
62	    }
63	    default:
64	      return [];
65	  }
66	}
67	
68	function encodeFloat(value: number, dataType: string, bigEndian: boolean): number[] {
69	  const le = !bigEndian;
70	  if (dataType === 'float') {
71	    const buf = new ArrayBuffer(4);
72	    new DataView(buf).setFloat32(0, value, le);
73	    return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
74	  }
75	  const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8);
76	  new DataView(buf).setFloat64(0, value, le);
77	  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)];
78	}
79	
80	function encodeBytes(value: SendFieldValue, length: number): number[] {
81	  const str = typeof value === 'string' ? value : String(value);
82	  const hex = str.replace(/^0x/i, '').replace(/\s/g, '');
83	  const bytes: number[] = [];
84	  for (let i = 0; i < hex.length && bytes.length < length; i += 2) {
85	    const byte = parseInt(hex.substring(i, i + 2), 16);
86	    if (!isNaN(byte)) bytes.push(byte);
87	  }
88	  while (bytes.length < length) bytes.push(0);
89	  return bytes.slice(0, length);
90	}
91	
92	function encodeASCII(value: SendFieldValue, length: number): number[] {
93	  const str = String(value);
94	  const bytes: number[] = [];
95	  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
96	    bytes.push(i < str.length ? str.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff : 0);
97	  }
98	  return bytes;
99	}
100	
101	function encodeFieldValue(
102	  value: SendFieldValue,
103	  field: SendFieldEncodingDef,
104	): number[] {
105	  if (field.isASCII) return encodeASCII(value, field.length);
106	  if (field.dataType === 'bytes') return encodeBytes(value, field.length);
107	  if (INTEGER_TYPES.has(field.dataType)) {
108	    return encodeInteger(parseNumericValue(value), field.dataType, field.bigEndian);
109	  }
110	  if (FLOAT_TYPES.has(field.dataType)) {
111	    return encodeFloat(parseNumericValue(value), field.dataType, field.bigEndian);
112	  }
113	  return [];
114	}
115	
116	export function buildFrame(input: SendBuildInput): FrameBuildOutput {
117	  const { fields, totalByteLength, fieldValues } = input;
118	  const buffer = new Uint8Array(totalByteLength);
119	  const resolvedFields: Record<string, ResolvedFieldValue> = {};
120	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
121	
122	  for (const field of fields) {
123	    const value = fieldValues[field.id];
124	    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') {
125	      issues.push({
126	        severity: 'warning',
127	        code: 'send.field.missingValue',
128	        fieldId: field.id,
129	        message: `Field "${field.id}" has no value, using zeros.`,
130	      });
131	      resolvedFields[field.id] = {
132	        fieldId: field.id,
133	        rawValue: 0,
134	        encodedBytes: Array(field.length).fill(0),
135	        offset: field.offset,
136	        length: field.length,
137	      };
138	      continue;
139	    }
140	
141	    const encoded = encodeFieldValue(value, field);
142	    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(encoded.length, field.length); i++) {
143	      buffer[field.offset + i] = encoded[i]!;
144	    }
145	
146	    resolvedFields[field.id] = {
147	      fieldId: field.id,
148	      rawValue: value,
149	      encodedBytes: encoded.slice(0, field.length),
150	      offset: field.offset,
151	      length: field.length,
152	    };
153	  }
154	
155	  return { bytes: buffer, resolvedFields, issues };
156	}
157

1	import { defaultNow } from '@/shared';
2	import { buildFrame } from '../core';
3	import type {
4	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
5	  SendBuildIssue,
6	  SendError,
7	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
8	  SendRequest,
9	  SendRequestRef,
10	  SendResult,
11	  SendResultKind,
12	  SendServiceStatus,
13	  SendStateSnapshot,
14	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
15	} from '../core';
16	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
17	import type {
18	  SendFrameReader,
19	  SendResultEmitter,
20	  SendTargetResolver,
21	  SendTransportWriteOutcome,
22	  SendTransportWriter,
23	} from '../adapters';
24	import { createSendState, type SendStateContainer } from '../state/send-state';
25	
26	export interface SendReader {
27	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
28	  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
29	  listResults(): SendResult[];
30	  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
31	}
32	
33	export interface SendService extends SendReader {
34	  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
35	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
36	}
37	
38	export interface CreateSendServiceOptions {
39	  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;
40	  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;
41	  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;
42	  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;
43	  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;
44	  readonly now?: () => string;
45	}
46	
47	function validateSendRequest(request: SendRequest): SendBuildIssue[] {
48	  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];
49	  if (!request.frameId) {
50	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingFrameId', message: 'frameId is required.' });
51	  }
52	  if (!request.targetId) {
53	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingTargetId', message: 'targetId is required.' });
54	  }
55	  if (!request.context?.source) {
56	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingSource', message: 'context.source is required.' });
57	  }
58	  return issues;
59	}
60	
61	function toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {
62	  return {
63	    frameId: request.frameId,
64	    targetId: request.targetId,
65	    context: { ...request.context },
66	  };
67	}
68	
69	function makeResult(
70	  kind: SendResultKind,
71	  requestRef: SendRequestRef,
72	  timestamp: string,
73	  bytesBuilt: number,
74	  bytesSent: number,
75	  error?: SendError,
76	  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],
77	): SendResult {
78	  return {
79	    kind,
80	    requestRef,
81	    bytesBuilt,
82	    bytesSent,
83	    timestamp,
84	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
85	    buildIssues,
86	  };
87	}
88	
89	function frameToBuildInput(
90	  frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset,
91	): { fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[]; totalByteLength: number } {
92	  const fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[] = [];
93	  let offset = 0;
94	  const frameEndian = frame.options?.bigEndian ?? false;
95	
96	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
97	    fields.push({
98	      id: field.id,
99	      dataType: field.dataType,
100	      length: field.length,
101	      bigEndian: field.bigEndian ?? frameEndian,
102	      isASCII: field.isASCII ?? false,
103	      offset,
104	    });
105	    offset += field.length;
106	  }
107	
108	  return { fields, totalByteLength: offset };
109	}
110	
111	export function createSendReader(
112	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
113	): SendReader {
114	  return {
115	    getSnapshot() {
116	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider());
117	    },
118	    getStatistics() {
119	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().statistics);
120	    },
121	    listResults() {
122	      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().recentResults);
123	    },
124	    getStatus() {
125	      return snapshotProvider().status;
126	    },
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	export function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {
131	  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();
132	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
133	  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
134	
135	  return {
136	    ...reader,
137	
138	    async execute(request) {
139	      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);
140	      const timestamp = now();
141	
142	      state.setStatus('sending');
143	
144	      // 1. Validate request
145	      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);
146	      const hasErrors = validationIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
147	      if (hasErrors) {
148	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, validationIssues);
149	        state.addResult(result);
150	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
151	        return result;
152	      }
153	
154	      // 2. Resolve frame snapshot
155	      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);
156	      if (!frame) {
157	        const result = makeResult(
158	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
159	          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },
160	        );
161	        state.addResult(result);
162	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
163	        return result;
164	      }
165	
166	      // 3. Build frame bytes
167	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);
168	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
169	        fields,
170	        totalByteLength,
171	        fieldValues: request.fieldValues,
172	      });
173	
174	      const buildErrors = buildOutput.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
175	      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {
176	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildOutput.issues);
177	        state.addResult(result);
178	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
179	        return result;
180	      }
181	
182	      const bytes = [...buildOutput.bytes];
183	
184	      // 4. Resolve target
185	      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);
186	      if (!target) {
187	        const result = makeResult(
188	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
189	          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },
190	        );
191	        state.addResult(result);
192	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
193	        return result;
194	      }
195	
196	      if (!target.available) {
197	        const result = makeResult(
198	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
199	          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },
200	        );
201	        state.addResult(result);
202	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
203	        return result;
204	      }
205	
206	      // 5. Transport write
207	      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
208	      try {
209	        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytes);
210	      } catch (err) {
211	        const result = makeResult(
212	          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
213	          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },
214	          buildOutput.issues,
215	        );
216	        state.addResult(result);
217	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
218	        return result;
219	      }
220	
221	      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {
222	        const result = makeResult(
223	          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',
224	          requestRef,
225	          timestamp,
226	          bytes.length,
227	          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
228	          writeOutcome.error,
229	          buildOutput.issues,
230	        );
231	        state.addResult(result);
232	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
233	        return result;
234	      }
235	
236	      // 6. Success
237	      const result = makeResult(
238	        'sent',
239	        requestRef,
240	        timestamp,
241	        bytes.length,
242	        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
243	        undefined,
244	        buildOutput.issues,
245	      );
246	      state.addResult(result);
247	      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
248	      return result;
249	    },
250	
251	    resetStats() {
252	      return state.resetStats();
253	    },
254	  };
255	}
256

现在我清楚地了解了当前状态。`frame-resolver.ts` 已经有完整的 `frameToBuildInput`，其中包含 `factor`、`configurable`、`expressionConfig`、`validOption` 等字段，但是 `send-service.ts` 有一个本地的 `frameToBuildInput`，没有包含这些字段，并且没有在构建/发送之前解析字段值、应用表达式或应用后置补丁。

集成测试期望 `send-service`：
1. 通过 `frameToBuildInput` 将帧转换为编码定义
2. 通过 `resolveFieldValues` 解析字段值（表达式、用户值、默认值）
3. 通过 `applyFactor` 应用因子
4. 构建帧字节
5. 应用构建后置补丁（校验和、长度）
6. 解析目标并发送

当前 `send-service.ts` 使用它自己的精简版 `frameToBuildInput`，并且缺少步骤 2、3、5。

让我再检查一下 `FrameAsset` 类型，以了解字段结构。

14	export function createFakeConnectionWriter(

18	  for (const t of options.targets ?? []) {

32	    },
33

35	      recordedWrites.push({ connectionId, bytes: [...bytes] });

37	    },
38	  };
39	}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/core/checksum.ts",
  "content": "import type { SendBuildIssue, SendFieldEncodingDef, SendOptions } from './types';\n\nexport function checksumSum8(bytes: readonly number[]): number {\n  let sum = 0;\n  for (const b of bytes) sum += b;\n  return sum & 0xff;\n}\n\nexport function checksumXor8(bytes: readonly number[]): number {\n  let result = 0;\n  for (const b of bytes) result ^= b;\n  return result & 0xff;\n}\n\nconst CRC16_MODBUS_TABLE = (() => {\n  const table: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {\n    let crc = i;\n    for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {\n      crc = crc & 1 ? (crc >>> 1) ^ 0xa001 : crc >>> 1;\n    }\n    table.push(crc);\n  }\n  return table;\n})();\n\nexport function checksumCrc16Modbus(bytes: readonly number[]): number {\n  let crc = 0xffff;\n  for (const b of bytes) {\n    crc = (crc >>> 8) ^ CRC16_MODBUS_TABLE[(crc ^ b) & 0xff]!;\n  }\n  return crc & 0xffff;\n}\n\nconst CRC32_TABLE = (() => {\n  const table: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {\n    let crc = i;\n    for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {\n      crc = crc & 1 ? (crc >>> 1) ^ 0xedb88320 : crc >>> 1;\n    }\n    table.push(crc >>> 0);\n  }\n  return table;\n})();\n\nexport function checksumCrc32(bytes: readonly number[]): number {\n  let crc = 0xffffffff;\n  for (const b of bytes) {\n    crc = (crc >>> 8) ^ CRC32_TABLE[(crc ^ b) & 0xff]!;\n  }\n  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;\n}\n\nexport interface ChecksumOptions {\n  readonly startIdx?: number;\n  readonly endIdx?: number;\n}\n\nexport function calculateChecksum(\n  bytes: readonly number[],\n  kind: string,\n  options?: ChecksumOptions,\n): number {\n  let slice = bytes;\n  if (options?.startIdx !== undefined || options?.endIdx !== undefined) {\n    slice = bytes.slice(options?.startIdx ?? 0, options?.endIdx);\n  }\n  switch (kind) {\n    case 'sum8':\n      return checksumSum8(slice);\n    case 'xor8':\n      return checksumXor8(slice);\n    case 'crc16':\n    case 'crc16-modbus':\n      return checksumCrc16Modbus(slice);\n    case 'crc32':\n      return checksumCrc32(slice);\n    default:\n      return 0;\n  }\n}\n\nexport interface BuildPatchResult {\n  readonly buffer: Uint8Array;\n  readonly issues: readonly SendBuildIssue[];\n}\n\nfunction makeIssue(\n  severity: SendBuildIssue['severity'],\n  code: string,\n  message: string,\n  fieldId?: string,\n): SendBuildIssue {\n  return { severity, code, fieldId, message };\n}\n\nexport function applyBuildPostPatch(\n  buffer: Uint8Array,\n  fields: readonly SendFieldEncodingDef[],\n  options?: SendOptions,\n): BuildPatchResult {\n  const result = new Uint8Array(buffer);\n  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];\n\n  if (!options) {\n    return { buffer: result, issues };\n  }\n\n  // Auto checksum\n  if (options.autoChecksum) {\n    for (const field of fields) {\n      if (!field.validOption?.isChecksum) continue;\n\n      const { startFieldIndex, endFieldIndex, checksumMethod } = field.validOption;\n\n      // Validate range\n      if (startFieldIndex >= fields.length || endFieldIndex >= fields.length) {\n        issues.push(makeIssue('error', 'send.build.invalidChecksumRange',\n          `Checksum field index range [${startFieldIndex}, ${endFieldIndex}] out of bounds.`, field.id));\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      // Check supported field lengths for checksum\n      if (field.length !== 1 && field.length !== 2 && field.length !== 4) {\n        issues.push(makeIssue('error', 'send.build.unsupportedChecksumFieldLength',\n          `Checksum field \"${field.id}\" has unsupported length ${field.length}.`, field.id));\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const method = checksumMethod ?? 'sum8';\n\n      // Collect bytes from startFieldIndex to endFieldIndex (inclusive)\n      const startByte = fields[startFieldIndex]!.offset;\n      const endField = fields[endFieldIndex]!;\n      const endByte = endField.offset + endField.length;\n      const checksumBytes = Array.from(result.slice(startByte, endByte));\n\n      const checksumValue = calculateChecksum(checksumBytes, method);\n\n      // Check overflow\n      const maxForLength = field.length === 1 ? 0xff : field.length === 2 ? 0xffff : 0xffffffff;\n      if (checksumValue > maxForLength) {\n        issues.push(makeIssue('error', 'send.build.checksumOverflow',\n          `Checksum value 0x${checksumValue.toString(16)} exceeds field capacity (${field.length} bytes).`, field.id));\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      // Write checksum\n      const be = field.bigEndian;\n      const view = new DataView(result.buffer, result.byteOffset + field.offset, field.length);\n      if (field.length === 1) {\n        result[field.offset] = checksumValue & 0xff;\n      } else if (field.length === 2) {\n        view.setUint16(0, checksumValue, !be);\n      } else if (field.length === 4) {\n        view.setUint32(0, checksumValue, !be);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Auto length field\n  if (options.includeLengthField) {\n    if (!options.lengthFieldId) {\n      issues.push(makeIssue('warning', 'send.build.lengthFieldIdMissing',\n        'includeLengthField is set but lengthFieldId is not specified.'));\n    } else {\n      const lengthField = fields.find((f) => f.id === options.lengthFieldId);\n      if (!lengthField) {\n        issues.push(makeIssue('warning', 'send.build.lengthFieldNotFound',\n          `Length field \"${options.lengthFieldId}\" not found in frame fields.`));\n      } else {\n        const totalLength = result.length;\n        const be = lengthField.bigEndian;\n        const view = new DataView(result.buffer, result.byteOffset + lengthField.offset, lengthField.length);\n        if (lengthField.length === 1) {\n          result[lengthField.offset] = totalLength & 0xff;\n        } else if (lengthField.length === 2) {\n          view.setUint16(0, totalLength, !be);\n        } else if (lengthField.length === 4) {\n          view.setUint32(0, totalLength, !be);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { buffer: result, issues };\n}\n"
}

'../core/frame-resolver';\nimport type {\n  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,\n  SendBuildIssue,\n  SendError,\n  SendFieldEncodingDef,\n  SendRequest,\n  SendRequestRef,\n  SendResult,\n  SendResultKind,\n  SendServiceStatus,\n  SendStateSnapshot,\n  SendStatisticsSnapshot,\n} from '../core';\nimport type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';\nimport type {\n  SendFrameReader,\n  SendResultEmitter,\n  SendTargetResolver,\n  SendTransportWriteOutcome,\n  SendTransportWriter,\n  SendVariableProvider,\n} from '../adapters';\nimport { createSendState, type SendStateContainer } from '../state/send-state';\n\nexport interface SendReader {\n  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;\n  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;\n  listResults(): SendResult[];\n  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;\n}\n\nexport interface SendService extends SendReader {\n  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;\n  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;\n}\n\nexport interface CreateSendServiceOptions {\n  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;\n  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;\n  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;\n  readonly variableProvider?: SendVariableProvider;\n  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;\n  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;\n  readonly now?: () => string;\n}\n\nfunction validateSendRequest(request: SendRequest): SendBuildIssue[] {\n  const issues: SendBuildIssue[] = [];\n  if (!request.frameId) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingFrameId', message: 'frameId is required.' });\n  }\n  if (!request.targetId) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingTargetId', message: 'targetId is required.' });\n  }\n  if (!request.context?.source) {\n    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'send.request.missingSource', message: 'context.source is required.' });\n  }\n  return issues;\n}\n\nfunction toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {\n  return {\n    frameId: request.frameId,\n    targetId: request.targetId,\n    context: { ...request.context },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction makeResult(\n  kind: SendResultKind,\n  requestRef: SendRequestRef,\n  timestamp: string,\n  bytesBuilt: number,\n  bytesSent: number,\n  error?: SendError,\n  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],\n): SendResult {\n  return {\n    kind,\n    requestRef,\n    bytesBuilt,\n    bytesSent,\n    timestamp,\n    ...(error ? { error } : {}),\n    buildIssues,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createSendReader(\n  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,\n): SendReader {\n  return {\n    getSnapshot() {\n      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider());\n    },\n    getStatistics() {\n      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().statistics);\n    },\n    listResults() {\n      return structuredClone(snapshotProvider().recentResults);\n    },\n    getStatus() {\n      return snapshotProvider().status;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction noOpVariableProvider(): SendVariableProvider {\n  return { getVariables: () => new Map() };\n}\n\nexport function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {\n  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();\n  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;\n  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());\n  const variableProvider = options.variableProvider ?? noOpVariableProvider();\n\n  return {\n    ...reader,\n\n    async execute(request) {\n      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);\n      const timestamp = now();\n\n      state.setStatus('sending');\n\n      // 1. Validate request\n      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);\n      const hasErrors = validationIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');\n      if (hasErrors) {\n        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, validationIssues);\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      // 2. Resolve frame snapshot\n      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);\n      if (!frame) {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,\n          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      // 3. Direction check\n      if (frame.direction === 'receive') {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,\n          { kind: 'wrong-direction', message: `Frame \"${request.frameId}\" is receive-only.` },\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      // 4. Convert frame to encoding defs and resolve field values\n      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);\n      const userValues = request.userFieldValues ?? request.fieldValues ?? {};\n      const resolved = resolveFieldValues(fields, userValues, variableProvider);\n      const factored = applyFactor(fields, resolved.values);\n      const allIssues = [...resolved.issues, ...factored.issues];\n\n      // 5. Build frame bytes\n      const buildOutput = buildFrame({\n        fields,\n        totalByteLength,\n        fieldValues: factored.values,\n      });\n      allIssues.push(...buildOutput.issues);\n\n      const buildErrors = allIssues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');\n      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {\n        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, allIssues);\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      let bytes = buildOutput.bytes;\n\n      // 6. Apply post-build patch (checksum, length)\n      const frameOptions = frame.options;\n      const patchOptions = request.options ?? (frameOptions ? {\n        autoChecksum: frameOptions.autoChecksum,\n        bigEndian: frameOptions.bigEndian,\n        includeLengthField: frameOptions.includeLengthField,\n      } : undefined);\n      const patched = applyBuildPostPatch(bytes, fields, patchOptions);\n      allIssues.push(...patched.issues);\n\n      const patchErrors = allIssues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');\n      if (patchErrors.length > 0) {\n        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, allIssues);\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      bytes = patched.buffer;\n      const bytesArr = [...bytes];\n\n      // 7. Resolve target\n      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);\n      if (!target) {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,\n          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      if (!target.available) {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,\n          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      // 8. Transport write\n      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;\n      try {\n        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytesArr);\n      } catch (err) {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytesArr.length, 0,\n          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },\n          allIssues,\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {\n        const result = makeResult(\n          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',\n          requestRef,\n          timestamp,\n          bytesArr.length,\n          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,\n          writeOutcome.error,\n          allIssues,\n        );\n        state.addResult(result);\n        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n        return result;\n      }\n\n      // 9. Success\n      const result = makeResult(\n        'sent',\n        requestRef,\n        timestamp,\n        bytesArr.length,\n        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,\n        undefined,\n        allIssues,\n      );\n      state.addResult(result);\n      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);\n      return result;\n    },\n\n    resetStats() {\n      return state.resetStats();\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

```
1	import type { WaitCondition, ConditionMatchInput } from './types';
2	import { compareValues } from '@/shared/condition-operators';
3	
4	export function evaluateCondition(
5	  condition: WaitCondition,
6	  input: ConditionMatchInput,
7	): boolean {
8	  if (input.frameId !== condition.frameId || input.fieldId !== condition.fieldId) {
9	    return false;
10	  }
11	  if (condition.sourceId !== undefined) {
12	    if (input.sourceId === undefined || condition.sourceId !== input.sourceId) {
13	      return false;
14	    }
15	  }
16	  return compareValues(input.value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
17	}
18	
```

```
1	import type { WaitCondition, ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import { evaluateCondition } from '../core';
3	
4	// --- Condition registry ---
5	
6	export interface ConditionEntry {
7	  readonly condition: WaitCondition;
8	  readonly onMatch: (value: number | string) => void;
9	}
10	
11	export class ConditionRegistry {
12	  private entries = new Map<string, Set<ConditionEntry>>();
13	
14	  register(condition: WaitCondition, onMatch: (value: number | string) => void): ConditionEntry {
15	    const entry: ConditionEntry = { condition, onMatch };
16	    const key = condition.frameId;
17	    if (!this.entries.has(key)) {
18	      this.entries.set(key, new Set());
19	    }
20	    this.entries.get(key)!.add(entry);
21	    return entry;
22	  }
23	
24	  unregister(entry: ConditionEntry): void {
25	    const key = entry.condition.frameId;
26	    const set = this.entries.get(key);
27	    if (set) {
28	      set.delete(entry);
29	      if (set.size === 0) {
30	        this.entries.delete(key);
31	      }
32	    }
33	  }
34	
35	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
36	    const set = this.entries.get(input.frameId);
37	    if (!set) return;
38	    for (const entry of set) {
39	      if (evaluateCondition(entry.condition, input)) {
40	        entry.onMatch(input.value as number | string);
41	      }
42	    }
43	  }
44	
45	  clear(): void {
46	    this.entries.clear();
47	  }
48	
49	  get size(): number {
50	    let total = 0;
51	    for (const set of this.entries.values()) {
52	      total += set.size;
53	    }
54	    return total;
55	  }
56	}
57	
```

测试期望与实际代码在结构上存在差异。我需要：

1. 在 `types` 中添加 `ConditionTerm` 类型
2. 更新 `ConditionMatchInput` 以支持基于 `fieldValues` 的格式（或者测试使用了不同的接口）
3. 在 `condition-matcher` 中添加 `evaluateSingleCondition` 和 `evaluateConditionGroup`
4. 重写 `ConditionRegistry` 以支持基于分组的 `API`

让我仔细检查测试期望什么 `ConditionMatchInput` 接口。测试中包含：
```
function input(frameId, fieldValues, sourceId?): ConditionMatchInput
```
因此测试期望 `ConditionMatchInput` 是 `{ frameId, fieldValues, sourceId? }`。

  "content": "import type { WaitCondition, ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from './types';\nimport { compareValues } from '@/shared/condition-operators';\n\nexport function evaluateCondition(\n  condition: WaitCondition,\n  input: ConditionMatchInput,\n): boolean {\n  if (input.frameId !== condition.frameId) {\n    return false;\n  }\n  if (condition.sourceId !== undefined) {\n    if (input.sourceId === undefined || condition.sourceId !== input.sourceId) {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  const value = input.fieldValues[condition.fieldId];\n  if (value === undefined) return false;\n  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);\n}\n\nexport function evaluateSingleCondition(\n  condition: ConditionTerm,\n  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,\n): boolean {\n  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;\n  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);\n}\n\nexport function evaluateConditionGroup(\n  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],\n  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,\n): boolean {\n  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;\n\n  let result = true;\n  for (let i = 0; i < conditions.length; i++) {\n    const matches = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i]!, fieldValues);\n    const logicOp = i === 0 ? 'and' : (conditions[i]!.logicOperator ?? 'and');\n\n    if (logicOp === 'or') {\n      result = result || matches;\n      if (result) return true;\n    } else {\n      result = i === 0 ? matches : result && matches;\n      if (!result && logicOp === 'and') return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/services/condition-registry.ts",
  "content": "import type { ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';\nimport { evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';\n\nexport interface ConditionGroup {\n  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];\n  readonly callback: () => void;\n}\n\nexport class ConditionRegistry {\n  private groups: ConditionGroup[] = [];\n  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<number>>();\n\n  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], callback: () => void): ConditionGroup {\n    const group: ConditionGroup = { conditions, callback };\n    const idx = this.groups.length;\n    this.groups.push(group);\n\n    for (const cond of conditions) {\n      if (!this.frameIndex.has(cond.frameId)) {\n        this.frameIndex.set(cond.frameId, new Set());\n      }\n      this.frameIndex.get(cond.frameId)!.add(idx);\n    }\n    return group;\n  }\n\n  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {\n    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(group);\n    if (idx === -1) return;\n    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);\n    this.rebuildIndex();\n  }\n\n  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {\n    const candidateIndices = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);\n    if (!candidateIndices) return;\n\n    for (const idx of candidateIndices) {\n      const group = this.groups[idx];\n      if (!group) continue;\n\n      // Check sourceId filter\n      const hasSourceFilter = group.conditions.some((c) => c.sourceId !== undefined);\n      if (hasSourceFilter && input.sourceId !== undefined) {\n        const anyMatch = group.conditions.some(\n          (c) => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId,\n        );\n        if (!anyMatch) continue;\n      }\n      // If input has no sourceId but conditions require sourceId, skip sourceId check\n      // (filter is skipped when input has no sourceId)\n\n      if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, { ...input.fieldValues })) {\n        group.callback();\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  clear(): void {\n    this.groups = [];\n    this.frameIndex.clear();\n  }\n\n  get size(): number {\n    return this.groups.length;\n  }\n\n  private rebuildIndex(): void {\n    this.frameIndex.clear();\n    for (let i = 0; i < this.groups.length; i++) {\n      for (const cond of this.groups[i]!.conditions) {\n        if (!this.frameIndex.has(cond.frameId)) {\n          this.frameIndex.set(cond.frameId, new Set());\n        }\n        this.frameIndex.get(cond.frameId)!.add(i);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
}

1	import type { ComparisonOperator } from './types';
2	
3	export function compareValues(actual: unknown, threshold: unknown, operator: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
4	  if (operator === 'any') {
5	    return actual != null;
6	  }
7	
8	  if (operator === 'change') {
9	    return actual != null && threshold != null && actual !== threshold;
10	  }
11	
12	  if (operator === 'contains') {
13	    if (actual == null || threshold == null) return false;
14	    return String(actual).includes(String(threshold));
15	  }
16	
17	  if (actual == null || threshold == null) {
18	    return false;
19	  }
20	
21	  const actualNum = toNumber(actual);
22	  const thresholdNum = toNumber(threshold);
23	
24	  const useNumeric = actualNum != null && thresholdNum != null;
25	
26	  if (useNumeric) {
27	    return numericCompare(actualNum, thresholdNum, operator);
28	  }
29	
30	  return stringCompare(String(actual), String(threshold), operator);
31	}
32	
33	function toNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
34	  if (typeof value === 'number') {
35	    return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
36	  }
37	  if (typeof value === 'string') {
38	    if (value.startsWith('0x') || value.startsWith('0X')) {
39	      const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 16);
40	      return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
41	    }
42	    const parsed = Number(value);
43	    return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
44	  }
45	  if (typeof value === 'bigint') {
46	    return Number(value);
47	  }
48	  if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
49	    return value ? 1 : 0;
50	  }
51	  return null;
52	}
53	
54	function numericCompare(a: number, b: number, op: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
55	  switch (op) {
56	    case 'eq': return a === b;
57	    case 'neq': return a !== b;
58	    case 'gt': return a > b;
59	    case 'lt': return a < b;
60	    case 'gte': return a >= b;
61	    case 'lte': return a <= b;
62	    default: return false;
63	  }
64	}
65	
66	function stringCompare(a: string, b: string, op: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
67	  switch (op) {
68	    case 'eq': return a === b;
69	    case 'neq': return a !== b;
70	    case 'gt': return a > b;
71	    case 'lt': return a < b;
72	    case 'gte': return a >= b;
73	    case 'lte': return a <= b;
74	    default: return false;
75	  }

1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';

4	export interface FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions {
5	  readonly autoEmit?: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
6	  readonly autoEmitIntervalMs?: number;
7	}
8	
9	export function createFakeReceiveEventSource(): ReceiveEventSource & {
10	  emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void;
11	  readonly emitted: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
12	} {
13	  const emitted: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
14	  const handlers = new Set<(input: ConditionMatchInput) => void>();
15	
16	  return {
17	    get emitted() {
18	      return emitted;
19	    },
20	
21	    emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
22	      emitted.push(input);
23	      for (const handler of handlers) {
24	        handler(input);
25	      }
26	    },
27	
28	    subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {
29	      handlers.add(handler);
30	      return () => {
31	        handlers.delete(handler);
32	      };
33	    },
34	  };
35	}

1	import type { WaitCondition, ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from './types';
2	import { compareValues } from '@/shared/condition-operators';
3	
4	export function evaluateCondition(
5	  condition: WaitCondition,
6	  input: ConditionMatchInput,
7	): boolean {
8	  if (input.frameId !== condition.frameId) {
9	    return false;
10	  }
11	  if (condition.sourceId !== undefined) {
12	    if (input.sourceId === undefined || condition.sourceId !== input.sourceId) {
13	      return false;
14	    }
15	  }
16	  const value = input.fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
17	  if (value === undefined) return false;
18	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
19	}
20	
21	export function evaluateSingleCondition(
22	  condition: ConditionTerm,
23	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
24	): boolean {
25	  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
26	  if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;
27	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
28	}
29	
30	export function evaluateConditionGroup(
31	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],
32	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
33	): boolean {
34	  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
35	
36	  let result = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[0]!, fieldValues);
37	
38	  for (let i = 1; i < conditions.length; i++) {
39	    const matches = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i]!, fieldValues);
40	    const logicOp = conditions[i]!.logicOperator ?? 'and';
41	
42	    if (logicOp === 'or') {
43	      result = result || matches;
44	      if (result) return true;
45	    } else {
46	      result = result && matches;
47	      if (!result) return false;
48	    }
49	  }
50	  return result;
51	}
52	
```

> AGENT

第17行检查 `value === undefined` - 如果字段在 `fieldValues` 中不存在，则返回 `false`。但是如果 `value` 是 `null`，它会通过到 `compareValues(null, threshold, 'any')`，现在返回 `true`。很好。

但是第25-26行的 `evaluateSingleCondition` 检查 `value === null || value === undefined` 在 `compareValues` 之前 - 这意味着 `any` 对 `null` 不会通过 `evaluateSingleCondition`。让我检查一下这是否被测试了……`ta[REDACTED_SK]` 测试没有测试 `any` + `null`。`task-core` 测试通过 `evaluateCondition` 测试了它。因此 `evaluateSingleCondition` 应该没问题。

1	import type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '../core';
2	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
3	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
4	import type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
5	import type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
6	import { sleep } from './task-step-executors';
7	import type { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
8	
9	// --- Iteration loop context ---
10	
11	export interface IterationLoopContext {
12	  state: TaskStateContainer;
13	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
14	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
15	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
16	  errorPolicy: ReturnType<typeof createErrorPolicyHandler>;
17	  now: () => string;
18	}
19	
20	export function createIterationLoops(ctx: IterationLoopContext) {
21	  // --- executeStepCore ---
22	
23	  async function executeStepCore(
24	    instanceId: string,
25	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
26	    iteration: number,
27	    stepIndex: number,
28	    definition: TaskDefinition,
29	    signal: Promise<void>,
30	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
31	    switch (step.kind) {
32	      case 'send': {
33	        const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
34	        if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
35	          return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
36	        }
37	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
38	      }
39	
40	      case 'wait-condition': {
41	        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(
42	          instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
43	        );
44	        if (interrupted) return null;
45	
46	        if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {
47	          switch (step.waitConfig.onTimeout) {
48	            case 'continue':
49	              return { result, shouldStop: false };
50	            case 'skip':
51	              return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
52	            case 'fail':
53	              return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
54	          }
55	        }
56	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
57	      }
58	
59	      case 'delay': {
60	        const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(
61	          iteration, stepIndex, step.delayConfig.durationMs, signal,
62	        );
63	        if (interrupted) return null;
64	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
65	      }
66	    }
67	  }
68	
69	  // --- executeSteps ---
70	
71	  async function executeSteps(
72	    instanceId: string,
73	    iteration: number,
74	    steps: readonly TaskDefinition['steps'],
75	    definition: TaskDefinition,
76	    signal: Promise<void>,
77	  ): Promise<boolean> {
78	    const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
79	    if (!inst) return false;
80	    const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
81	      .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
82	      .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
83	    const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
84	
85	    for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
86	      const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
87	      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
88	
89	      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
90	
91	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(
92	        instanceId, steps[i], iteration, i, definition, signal,
93	      );
94	
95	      if (outcome === null) return false;
96	
97	      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
98	
99	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
100	    }
101	    return true;
102	  }
103	
104	  // --- Scheduling loops ---
105	
106	  async function runTimedLoop(
107	    instanceId: string,
108	    definition: TaskDefinition,
109	    signal: Promise<void>,
110	  ): Promise<void> {
111	    const { intervalMs, delayBeforeStartMs } = definition;
112	    const maxIter = definition.stopCondition?.maxIterations ?? Infinity;
113	    const maxDurationMs = definition.stopCondition?.maxDurationMs;
114	    const startTime = Date.now();
115	    const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
116	    const startIteration = inst?.currentIteration ?? 0;
117	
118	    if (delayBeforeStartMs && delayBeforeStartMs > 0 && startIteration === 0) {
119	      const startDelay = sleep(delayBeforeStartMs);
120	      const race = await Promise.race([
121	        startDelay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
122	        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
123	      ]);
124	      if (race === 'interrupted') {
125	        startDelay.cancel();
126	        return;
127	      }
128	    }
129	
130	    for (let iter = startIteration; iter < maxIter; iter++) {
131	      if (maxDurationMs && Date.now() - startTime >= maxDurationMs) break;
132	
133	      const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
134	      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
135	
136	      const completed = await executeSteps(
137	        instanceId, iter, definition.steps, definition, signal,
138	      );
139	
140	      if (!completed) return;
141	
142	      const afterExec = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
143	      if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
144	
145	      // Not last iteration → wait for interval
146	      if (iter + 1 < maxIter) {
147	        const intervalDelay = sleep(intervalMs ?? 1000);
148	        const race = await Promise.race([
149	          intervalDelay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
150	          signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
151	        ]);
152	        if (race === 'interrupted') {
153	          intervalDelay.cancel();
154	          return;
155	        }
156	      }
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  async function runTriggerLoop(
161	    instanceId: string,
162	    definition: TaskDefinition,
163	    signal: Promise<void>,
164	  ): Promise<void> {
165	    const triggerCondition = definition.triggerCondition;
166	    if (!triggerCondition) return;
167	
168	    const maxTriggerCount = definition.stopCondition?.maxIterations ?? Infinity;
169	    const maxDurationMs = definition.stopCondition?.maxDurationMs;
170	    const startTime = Date.now();
171	    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;
172	    let triggerCount = 0;
173	    let lastTriggerTime = 0;
174	    const triggerQueue: (number | string)[] = [];
175	    let queueNotify: (() => void) | null = null;
176	
177	    const handler = (_value: number | string) => {
178	      const currentTime = Date.now();
179	      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
180	      if (triggerCount + triggerQueue.length >= maxTriggerCount) return;
181	
182	      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;
183	      triggerQueue.push(_value);
184	      if (queueNotify) {
185	        queueNotify();
186	        queueNotify = null;
187	      }
188	    };
189	
190	    const entry = ctx.conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);
191	
192	    try {
193	      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {
194	        if (maxDurationMs && Date.now() - startTime >= maxDurationMs) break;
195	
196	        const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
197	        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
198	
199	        // Wait for queued trigger
200	        if (triggerQueue.length === 0) {
201	          const notified = await Promise.race([
202	            new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
203	              queueNotify = () => resolve(true);
204	            }),
205	            signal.then(() => false),
206	          ]);
207	          if (!notified) break;
208	        }
209	
210	        triggerQueue.shift();
211	        triggerCount++;
212	
213	        await executeSteps(
214	          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,
215	        );
216	
217	        const afterExec = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
218	        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
219	      }
220	    } finally {
221	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
222	    }
223	  }
224	
225	  async function runSequenceLoop(
226	    instanceId: string,
227	    definition: TaskDefinition,
228	    signal: Promise<void>,
229	  ): Promise<void> {
230	    await executeSteps(instanceId, 0, definition.steps, definition, signal);
231	  }
232	
233	  return { runTimedLoop, runTriggerLoop, runSequenceLoop };
234	}
235

1	import type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '../core';
2	import { isStepResultFailed } from '../core';
3	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
4	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
5	import type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
6	import type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
7	import { sleep } from './task-step-executors';
8	
9	// --- Error policy context ---
10	
11	export interface ErrorPolicyContext {
12	  state: TaskStateContainer;
13	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
14	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
15	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
16	  now: () => string;
17	}
18	
19	export function createErrorPolicyHandler(ctx: ErrorPolicyContext) {
20	  async function applyErrorPolicy(
21	    instanceId: string,
22	    failedResult: TaskStepResult,
23	    policy: TaskDefinition['errorPolicy'],
24	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
25	    iteration: number,
26	    stepIndex: number,
27	    definition: TaskDefinition,
28	    signal: Promise<void>,
29	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
30	    switch (policy.onFailure) {
31	      case 'stop':
32	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
33	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'stop' }, shouldStop: true };
34	
35	      case 'pause':
36	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
37	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'pause' }, shouldStop: true };
38	
39	      case 'skip-step':
40	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
41	
42	      case 'retry': {
43	        const maxRetries = policy.retryCount ?? 0;
44	        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
45	          if (policy.retryDelayMs && policy.retryDelayMs > 0) {
46	            const retryDelay = sleep(policy.retryDelayMs);
47	            const race = await Promise.race([
48	              retryDelay.promise.then(() => false),
49	              signal.then(() => true),
50	            ]);
51	            if (race) {
52	              retryDelay.cancel();
53	              return null;
54	            }
55	          }
56	
57	          const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
58	          if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;
59	
60	          // Re-execute raw step without re-entering error policy
61	          let retryResult: TaskStepResult;
62	          switch (step.kind) {
63	            case 'send':
64	              retryResult = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
65	              break;
66	            case 'wait-condition': {
67	              const wc = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal);
68	              if (wc.interrupted) return null;
69	              retryResult = wc.result;
70	              break;
71	            }
72	            case 'delay': {
73	              const d = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(iteration, stepIndex, step.delayConfig.durationMs, signal);
74	              if (d.interrupted) return null;
75	              retryResult = d.result;
76	              break;
77	            }
78	          }
79	
80	          if (!isStepResultFailed(retryResult)) {
81	            return { result: retryResult, shouldStop: false };
82	          }
83	        }
84	        // Exhausted retries
85	        ctx.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error: 'Retry exhausted' });
86	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'retry' }, shouldStop: true };
87	      }
88	    }
89	  }
90	
91	  return { applyErrorPolicy };
92	}
93

> AGENT

`sequenceTaskDef` 中有一个 `delayStep`，时长为 500 毫秒。但 427 行的测试重新定义了步骤：
```
steps: [
  { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
  { id: 's2', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
],
errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'retry', retryCount: 2, retryDelayMs: 10 },
```

所以没有延迟步骤——只有两个发送步骤。流程应该是：
1. s1：第一次尝试失败 → 错误策略重试 → 第二次尝试成功 → 继续
2. s2：第一次尝试成功 → 继续
3. 两个步骤完成 → 循环完成 → completeTask

但是 `retryDelayMs: 10`，测试有 2000ms 的超时。错误策略处理程序重试 `stepExecutors.executeSendStep`，它使用 `sendService.execute`。我来检查一下假 `sendService`。

unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {\n    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(group._group);\n    if (idx === -1) return;\n    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);\n    this.rebuildIndex();\n  }\n\n 21	function createTestSetup(overrides: { definition?: TaskDefinition; sendResults?: readonly SendResult[] } = {}) {
22	  const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results: overrides.sendResults ?? [makeSentResult()] });
23	  const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();

26	    receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
27	    now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',

30	  const instance = service.createTask(definition);
31	  return { service, fakeSend, fakeReceive, definition, instance };

34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}
